61: function normalizeHeaderText(value) {
62:   return String(value ?? "")
63:     .replace(/\r?\n/g, " ")
64:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
65:     .trim();
66: }
67: 
68: function detectHeaderRowIndex(rows) {
69:   const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
70:   const scanLimit = Math.min(source.length, 12);
71:   let bestIndex = -1;
72:   let bestScore = -Infinity;
73: 
74:   for (let index = 0; index < scanLimit; index += 1) {
75:     const cells = (source[index] || []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
76:     const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
77:     if (nonEmpty.length < 2) continue;
78: 
79:     const uniqueCount = new Set(nonEmpty).size;
80:     const duplicatePenalty = nonEmpty.length - uniqueCount;
81:     const knownHeaderHits = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
82:       /^(학생ID|학번|성명|사용성명|학년|반|번호|일자|운영일|일정ID|프로그램ID|강좌ID|기록ID|상태|구분|설정키|파일구분|스프레드시트ID|탭명|탭용도)$/.test(value),
83:     ).length;
84:     const titlePenalty = nonEmpty.length <= 2 && /UEP|표준|시트|DB|관리|기준/.test(nonEmpty.join(" ")) ? 8 : 0;
85:     const score = nonEmpty.length * 3 + uniqueCount + knownHeaderHits * 8 - duplicatePenalty * 2 - titlePenalty;
86: 
87:     if (score > bestScore) {
88:       bestScore = score;

68: function detectHeaderRowIndex(rows) {
69:   const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
70:   const scanLimit = Math.min(source.length, 12);
71:   let bestIndex = -1;

78: 
79:     const uniqueCount = new Set(nonEmpty).size;
80:     const duplicatePenalty = nonEmpty.length - uniqueCount;
81:     const knownHeaderHits = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>

83:     ).length;

85:     const score = nonEmpty.length * 3 + uniqueCount + knownHeaderHits * 8 - duplicatePenalty * 2 - titlePenalty;
86: 
87:     if (score > bestScore) {
88:       bestScore = score;

216:   );
217:   if (hasUsableHeaders) return normal;

220:   // 정규화 데이터가 직접 기록되는 구조가 되었습니다. 이 경우 헤더 자동감지 대신

225:     "사유", "목적지", "보호자확인", "비고", "처리상태", "응답일시", "처리일시",

236:     })

238: }

240: function serialToDate(value) {
241:   const serial = Number(value);
242:   if (!Number.isFinite(serial) || serial < 20000 || serial > 80000) return "";
243:   const utc = new Date(Math.round((serial - 25569) * 86400 * 1000));
244:   return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
245: }
246: 
247: function localDate(value) {
248:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
249:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
250:     return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
251:   const serialDate = serialToDate(value);
252:   if (serialDate) return serialDate;
253:   const text = String(value).trim();
254:   // __UEP_08159_SCHOOL_READ_DATE_CANONICAL__
255:   if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(text)){
256:     const instant=new Date(text);
257:     if(!Number.isNaN(instant.getTime())){
258:       const parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts(instant);
259:       const p=Object.fromEntries(parts.map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
260:       if(p.year&&p.month&&p.day)return p.year+'-'+p.month+'-'+p.day;
261:     }
262:   }
263:   const match = text.match(/(\d{4})[.\-/년]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\-/월]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
264:   if (match) return `${match[1]}-${match[2].padStart(2, "0")}-${match[3].padStart(2, "0")}`;
265:   const parsedText = new Date(text);
266:   if (!Number.isNaN(parsedText.getTime()) && /GMT|UTC|^[A-Za-z]{3}/.test(text))
267:     return `${parsedText.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(parsedText.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(parsedText.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
268:   const compact=text.replace(/\D/g,'');
269:   if(/^20\d{6}$/.test(compact))return compact.slice(0,4)+'-'+compact.slice(4,6)+'-'+compact.slice(6,8);
270:   const monthDay=text.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})\s*(?:월|[.\-/])\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*일)?(?:\D|$)/);
271:   if(monthDay)return '2026-'+monthDay[1].padStart(2,'0')+'-'+monthDay[2].padStart(2,'0');
272:   return text;
273: }
274: 
275: function localTime(value) {
276:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
277:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
278:     return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
279:   if (typeof value === "number") {
280:     const totalMinutes = Math.round((value % 1) * 24 * 60);
281:     return `${String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
282:   }
283:   const text = String(value).trim();
284:   if (/^0\.\d+$/.test(text)) return localTime(Number(text));
285:   const match = text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
286:   return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}` : text;
287: }

771:   const toMinutes = (value) => { const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null; };
772:   const slotRange = (row) => { const text = `${row.time || ""} ${row.type || ""} ${row.detail || ""}`; if (/오후자습|1타임/.test(text)) return [17*60,18*60,"오후자습"]; if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return [19*60+20,20*60+10,"야자1"]; if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return [20*60+20,21*60+30,"야자2"]; return null; };
773:   const programOverlaps = (program,row) => {

776:     // 강좌명이나 "오후자습" 문구만으로 방과후를 추정하지 않는다.
777:     const clocks=String(program.time||"").match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g)||[];
778:     if(clocks.length<2) return false;
779:     const start=toMinutes(clocks[0]),end=toMinutes(clocks[1]);
780:     return Number.isFinite(start)&&Number.isFinite(end)&&start<slot[1]&&end>slot[0];
781:   };

871: async function clearSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range) {
872:   const url = `https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:clear`;
873:   const response = await fetch(url, {
874:     method: "POST",
875:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
876:     body: "{}",
877:   });
878:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공결 삭제 실패 (${response.status})`);
879:   return response.json();
880: }

882: function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
883:   const now=new Date().toISOString();
884:   const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
885:   const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
886:   const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
887:   const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
888:   const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
889:   return [[
890:     id,
891:     String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
892:     String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
893:     String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,
894:     String(payload.reasonText||payload.reason||""), String(payload.place||""),
895:     String(payload.evidenceRequired||"N"), String(payload.evidence||"불필요"),
896:     String(payload.signatureRequired||"N"), String(payload.signatureStatus||"불필요"),
897:     String(payload.registrar||"UEP 직접등록"), now, String(payload.editor||"UEP"), String(payload.note||""),
898:     unit, scope, String(payload.originalType||"공결"), String(payload.sourceTab||"UEP 직접등록"), String(payload.sourceRow||"")
899:   ]];
900: }

914:     if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
915:   }else{

918:     id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
919:   }

923:   liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
924:   const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
925:   return {ok:true,id,data};
926: }

882: function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
883:   const now=new Date().toISOString();
884:   const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
885:   const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
886:   const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
887:   const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
888:   const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
889:   return [[
890:     id,
891:     String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
892:     String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
893:     String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,
894:     String(payload.reasonText||payload.reason||""), String(payload.place||""),
895:     String(payload.evidenceRequired||"N"), String(payload.evidence||"불필요"),
896:     String(payload.signatureRequired||"N"), String(payload.signatureStatus||"불필요"),
897:     String(payload.registrar||"UEP 직접등록"), now, String(payload.editor||"UEP"), String(payload.note||""),
898:     unit, scope, String(payload.originalType||"공결"), String(payload.sourceTab||"UEP 직접등록"), String(payload.sourceRow||"")
899:   ]];
900: }

902: async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
903:   const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
904:   if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");

906:   const range="'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000";
907:   const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
908:   const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
909:   const rows=matrix.slice(1);
910:   let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
911:   let sheetRow=-1;
912:   if(id){
913:     const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
914:     if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
915:   }else{
916:     let max=0;
917:     rows.forEach(row=>{const m=String(row?.[0]||"").match(/^ATT-2026-(\\d+)$/);if(m)max=Math.max(max,Number(m[1]));});
918:     id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
919:   }

921:   if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
922:   else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
923:   liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
924:   const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
925:   return {ok:true,id,data};
926: }

902: async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
903:   const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
904:   if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
905:   const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
906:   const range="'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000";
907:   const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
908:   const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
909:   const rows=matrix.slice(1);
910:   let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
911:   let sheetRow=-1;
912:   if(id){
913:     const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
914:     if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
915:   }else{
916:     let max=0;
917:     rows.forEach(row=>{const m=String(row?.[0]||"").match(/^ATT-2026-(\\d+)$/);if(m)max=Math.max(max,Number(m[1]));});
918:     id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
919:   }
920:   const values=officialAttendanceRow(payload,id);
921:   if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
922:   else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
923:   liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
924:   const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
925:   return {ok:true,id,data};
926: }

1694: function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {

1697:   const date = new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)), Number(text.slice(4,6))-1, Number(text.slice(6,8)), 12, 0, 0);
1698:   return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;

2382: function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
2383:   let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
2384:   const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;

2388:     if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
2389:   }

2487: function uepComparableDate(value,basis=attendanceViewDate||dateKey(today)){
2488:   if(value==null||value==='')return '';
2489:   if(typeof value==='object')value=value.formattedValue||value.value||value.date||value.serial||'';
2490:   const text=String(value).trim();
2491:   // __UEP_08160_ISO_CALENDAR_DATE__
=== app/resources/app/gyomuon.js :: officialAttendance @ 2515 ===
2488:   if(value==null||value==='')return '';
2489:   if(typeof value==='object')value=value.formattedValue||value.value||value.date||value.serial||'';
2490:   const text=String(value).trim();
2491:   // __UEP_08160_ISO_CALENDAR_DATE__
2492:   if(/^20\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2}T.*(?:Z|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/i.test(text)){
2493:     const instant=new Date(text);
2494:     if(!Number.isNaN(instant.getTime())){
2495:       const parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts(instant);
2496:       const p=Object.fromEntries(parts.map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
2497:       if(p.year&&p.month&&p.day)return p.year+'-'+p.month+'-'+p.day;
2498:     }
2499:   }
2500:   if(/^\d{5}(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(text)){
2501:     const serial=Number(text),utc=new Date(Math.round((serial-25569)*86400000));
2502:     if(!Number.isNaN(utc.getTime()))return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
2503:   }
2504:   const full=text.match(/(20\d{2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})/);
2505:   if(full)return `${full[1]}-${full[2].padStart(2,'0')}-${full[3].padStart(2,'0')}`;
2506:   const compact=text.replace(/\D/g,'');
2507:   if(/^20\d{6}$/.test(compact))return compact.slice(0,4)+'-'+compact.slice(4,6)+'-'+compact.slice(6,8);
2508:   const monthDay=text.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})\s*(?:월|[.\-/])\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*일)?(?:\D|$)/);
2509:   if(monthDay){const year=String(basis||dateKey(today)).slice(0,4)||'2026';return year+'-'+monthDay[1].padStart(2,'0')+'-'+monthDay[2].padStart(2,'0');}
2510:   const parsed=new Date(text);
2511:   if(!Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()))return `${parsed.getFullYear()}-${String(parsed.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(parsed.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
2512:   return text.slice(0,10);
2513: }

9495: function studentAccessContext(student){
9496:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
9497:   const studentClass=String(student?.className||student?.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
9498:   const ownClass=String(scope.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
9499:   const role=currentRoleId();
9500:   const isAdmin=role==="admin";
9501:   const isOwnHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&ownClass&&studentClass&&String(Number(ownClass))===String(Number(studentClass));
9502:   const isOtherHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&!isOwnHomeroom;
9503:   const isReadOnlyLeadership=role==="vice_principal"||role==="principal";
9504:   return {role,isAdmin,isOwnHomeroom,isOtherHomeroom,isReadOnlyLeadership,canEditContact:!isReadOnlyLeadership&&(isAdmin||isOwnHomeroom)};
9505: }

2376: function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
2377: function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
2378:   let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
2379:   for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
2380:   return day;
2381: }
2382: function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
2383:   let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
2384:   const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
2385:   for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
2386:     d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
2387:     const key=dateKey(d);
2388:     if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
2389:   }
2390:   return day;
2391: }

7125: function timetableMasterWeekDates(value){
7126:   const base=new Date(`${String(value||dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
7127:   if(Number.isNaN(base.getTime()))return [];

7129:   base.setDate(base.getDate()+(day===0?-6:1-day));
7130:   return Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(base);d.setDate(base.getDate()+i);return d;});
7131: }

2482:   const metricCard=(label,rows,detail)=>`<article class="attendance-personal-metric"><small>${label}</small><b>${rows.length}<em>건</em></b><span>${detail(rows)}</span></article>`;

2484:   return `<div class="attendance-stat-page"><section class="attendance-stat-selector"><div><small>ATTENDANCE · PERSONAL INSIGHT</small><h3>${selectedStudent?`${escapeHtml(selectedStudent.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(selectedStudent.name)}`:"학생 개인 출결 현황판"}</h3><p>반별 비교 없이 학생 한 명의 공결·지각·야자출결 누적 기록과 날짜를 확인합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-stat-pickers"><label>반 선택<select id="attendanceStatClassFilter">${classOptions}</select></label><label>학생 선택<select id="attendanceStatStudentFilter"><option value="all">학생을 선택하세요</option>${studentOptions}</select></label></div></section>${selectedStudent?`<div class="attendance-personal-metrics">${metricCard("공결",official,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 최근 ${dates(r)[0]||"-"}`)}${metricCard("지각",late,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 최근 ${dates(r)[0]||"-"}`)}${metricCard("야자출결",night,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 학사 제외 · 실제 출석·프로그램만`)}</div><div class="attendance-personal-history-grid">${history("공결 기록",official,row=>`${row.type||"공결"} · ${row.period||"하루 전체"}`)}${history("지각 기록",late,row=>`${row.time||row.period||"지각"}`)}${history("야자출결 기록",night,row=>`${row.result||row.type||row.detail||"출결"}`)}</div>`:'<div class="query-empty attendance-stat-empty"><b>학생을 선택하면 개인 출결 통계가 표시됩니다.</b><small>공결 횟수와 날짜, 지각 횟수와 날짜, 야자출결 누적 현황을 한 화면에서 확인합니다.</small></div>'}</div>`;
2485: }
2486: 
2487: function uepComparableDate(value,basis=attendanceViewDate||dateKey(today)){
2488:   if(value==null||value==='')return '';
2489:   if(typeof value==='object')value=value.formattedValue||value.value||value.date||value.serial||'';
2490:   const text=String(value).trim();
2491:   // __UEP_08160_ISO_CALENDAR_DATE__
2492:   if(/^20\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2}T.*(?:Z|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/i.test(text)){
2493:     const instant=new Date(text);
2494:     if(!Number.isNaN(instant.getTime())){
2495:       const parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts(instant);
2496:       const p=Object.fromEntries(parts.map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
2497:       if(p.year&&p.month&&p.day)return p.year+'-'+p.month+'-'+p.day;
2498:     }
2499:   }
2500:   if(/^\d{5}(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(text)){
2501:     const serial=Number(text),utc=new Date(Math.round((serial-25569)*86400000));
2502:     if(!Number.isNaN(utc.getTime()))return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
2503:   }
2504:   const full=text.match(/(20\d{2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})/);
2505:   if(full)return `${full[1]}-${full[2].padStart(2,'0')}-${full[3].padStart(2,'0')}`;
2506:   const compact=text.replace(/\D/g,'');
2507:   if(/^20\d{6}$/.test(compact))return compact.slice(0,4)+'-'+compact.slice(4,6)+'-'+compact.slice(6,8);
2508:   const monthDay=text.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})\s*(?:월|[.\-/])\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*일)?(?:\D|$)/);
2509:   if(monthDay){const year=String(basis||dateKey(today)).slice(0,4)||'2026';return year+'-'+monthDay[1].padStart(2,'0')+'-'+monthDay[2].padStart(2,'0');}
2510:   const parsed=new Date(text);
2511:   if(!Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()))return `${parsed.getFullYear()}-${String(parsed.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(parsed.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
2512:   return text.slice(0,10);
2513: }
2514: function attendanceView() {
2515:   const officialRows = readonlyCache?.officialAttendance || [];
2516:   const lateRows = readonlyCache?.lateAttendance || [];
2517:   const nightRows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];
2518:   if(attendanceMode==="night"&&!isNightOperatingDay(attendanceViewDate)){
2519:     attendanceViewDate=nearestNightOperatingDay(attendanceViewDate);
2520:     nightViewMonth=attendanceViewDate.slice(0,7);
2521:   }

"active" : ""}">공결</button><button data-attendance-mode="late" class="${attendanceMode === "late" ? "active" : ""}">지각</button><button data-attendance-mode="night" class="${attendanceMode === "night" ? "active" : ""}">야자출결</button><button data-attendance-mode="statistics" class="${attendanceMode === "statistics" ? "active" : ""}">통계</button></div>`;
2523:   if(attendanceMode === "statistics") return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendancePersonalStatisticsView(officialRows,lateRows,nightRows)}</div>`;
2524:   const genericRows = (rows, mode) => {
2525:     if (!rows.length) return `<div class="empty-state data-card"><b>선택일에 ${mode} 기록이 없습니다.</b><span>${escapeHtml(attendanceViewDate)} 기준</span></div>`;
2526:     return `<div class="data-card" id="attendanceRows"><div class="data-head attendance-detail-grid"><span>일자</span><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>구분</span><span>시간·교시</span><span>사유·상태</span><span>확인</span></div>${rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date))).map(row => `<div class="data-row attendance-detail-grid attendance-row" data-search="${escapeHtml([row.date,row.studentNo,row.name,row.className,row.type,row.detail,row.reason,row.result].join(" ").toLowerCase())}"><time>${escapeHtml(row.date || "-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo || "-")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row.name || "-")} · ${escapeHtml(row.className || "-")}반</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.type || mode)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.time || row.period || "-")}</span><span title="${escapeHtml(row.sourceTab?`출처: ${row.sourceTab}`:"")}">${escapeHtml(row.reason || row.detail || row.result || "-")}${row.sourceTab?`<small class="attendance-source-tab">${escapeHtml(row.sourceTab)}</small>`:""}</span><span class="state ${String(row.result || row.guidance).includes("필요") || /결석|미입실|미제출/.test(String(row.result)) ? "warn" : ""}">${escapeHtml(row.result || row.guidance || "확인")}</span></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
2527:   };
2528:   if (attendanceMode === "official") {
2529:     const selectedDay = officialRows.filter(row => uepComparableDate(row.date||row.day||row.rawDate||row['일자']||row['출결일자'],attendanceViewDate) === attendanceViewDate);
2530:     const classes = Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1));
2531:     const classSummary = classes.map(classNo=>{
2532:       const rows=selectedDay.filter(row=>String(row.className||row.classNo||"")===classNo);
2533:       return {classNo,records:rows.length,students:new Set(rows.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size};
2534:     });

2536:     const rosterStudents=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(student=>officialClassFilter==="all"||String(classNumberOf(student))===String(officialClassFilter)).sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
2537:     const studentOptions=rosterStudents;
2538:     if(officialStudentFilter!=="all"&&!studentOptions.some(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===officialStudentFilter))officialStudentFilter="all";
2539:     const selected = officialStudentFilter === "all" ? classFiltered : classFiltered.filter(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===officialStudentFilter);

2541:     const classCards=`<div class="night-class-overview official-class-overview"><button data-official-class="all" class="${officialClassFilter==="all"?"active":""}"><small>전체</small><b>${selectedDay.length}<em>기록</em></b><span>${new Set(selectedDay.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}명</span></button>${classSummary.map(item=>`<button data-official-class="${item.classNo}" class="${officialClassFilter===item.classNo?"active":""}"><small>${item.classNo}반</small><b>${item.records}<em>기록</em></b><span>${item.students}명</span></button>`).join("")}</div>`;

2543:     const canManageOfficial=currentRoleId()==="admin";

2545:     const selectedStudent=officialStudentFilter==="all"?null:rosterStudents.find(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===officialStudentFilter);
